dino · partition policy experiments

No partitioned candidate meets the matched flat cell limit for the latest measured compiler. Flat remains the only measured policy within that limit.

ASAP7, fixed Brent–Kung/SN arithmetic and full ABC recipe. Flat references match compiler, source bytes, Liberty and SDC. More partitions are useful only while mapped cell count stays at or below the matched flat result. 1,000 cells is a soft size goal; predicted AIG nodes and word-level nodes are different units. External diagnostics are excluded from corpus headline averages.

Useful bodies have nonzero mapped gates; zero-gate boundary scaffolding is excluded. These are backend region sizes before shared normalization; reused bodies carry physical instance counts in the evidence. Program checks provide coverage, not equivalence proofs.

Boundary investigation · 20261009T165525-partition-data-cones

The two useful colors do not correspond to pipeline stages. Color 1 contains most pipeline registers and an ALU result selector; color 2 contains many ALU operators and forwarding logic. Raw interfaces carry signals in both directions. Generated bit-extraction helper modules hide unused packed-register bits.

ObservationCellsFlopsCritical path (ps)
Retained two-color measurement4386241461713.61
Wiring helpers inlined + dead-cell cleanup (diagnostic)4270735771713.61
Matched flat measurement419663577988.53

Cleanup changes only the normalized netlist: inline __livehd_abc_input_bits_* wiring helpers, then opt_clean. No ABC remapping. This is a diagnostic, not a replacement synthesis measurement or a formal equivalence claim. Headline counts and acceptance continue to use the original ledger row. Memory cell count is unchanged.

ABC structural hashing finds 55 repeated cell-output functions in the cleaned partitioned circuit versus 32 in flat; bounded FRAIG finds 187 versus 161. Thus direct repeated outputs account for only 26 additional detected sharing opportunities. This does not bound all missed sharing: SAT has a 500-conflict limit, constants are abstracted as independent inputs, buffers/inverters are excluded, subfunctions inside cells are not counted, and equal functions may need replication for timing. The ALU operator/selector cut directly blocks joint Boolean restructuring and technology mapping; the exact remaining 741-cell attribution is unresolved.

Post-mapping cone traversal

Backward traversal matches all 3,776 data/output endpoints after helper cleanup. The execution-to-memory unions grow by 251 cells (pipe A) and 226 cells (pipe B), and their maximum cell levels increase from 25 to 36. Source-bit sets are unchanged. Disjoint physical-cell accounting finds 12,004 flat versus 12,555 two-color cells feeding only these two register groups: +551, or 74.4% of the remaining +741 cells. This localizes the mapped footprint, rather than proving an exact optimization cause. Most execution bit cones shrink (161/201 pipe A; 158/202 pipe B) while their union grows. Cells private to one bit increase from 2,095 to 2,372 in A and 2,064 to 2,480 in B, evidence of a less shared mapped structure, not just bigger individual cones. The retained worst timing endpoint pipeB_ex_mem.reg_0[89] has fewer cone cells (3,674 to 3,635) but more levels (25 to 35). The raw measurement has 569 extra register endpoints; cleanup removes them while every matched cone metric stays identical.

Named register data pins and primary outputs; sequential outputs are boundaries. Groups are unions of matched endpoint cones. Cells shared between groups or endpoints appear in each cone, so deltas cannot be summed. Structural source support is connectivity, not Boolean influence. Levels count physical cells and do not estimate delay. Name matching is not an equivalence proof.
